# Agents · Q · part 4

957 entries, qa-explorer to qa-reviewer. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **qa-explorer**: Use this agent when the user wants to explore a running web application to find bugs. This agent navigates the app via browser automation, interacts with UI elements, monitors console and network for errors, and reports raw findings. &lt;example&gt; Context: User has a running web app and wants to find bugs. user: "Explore http://localhost:3000 and find bugs" assistant: "I'll launch the qa-explorer agent to navigate the app, interact with UI elements, monitor console and network errors, and report any bugs found." &lt;commentary&gt; User explicitly asks to explore a running app for bugs. The explorer age… ([WilliamFontaine/claude-plugins-marketplace](https://github.com/WilliamFontaine/claude-plugins-marketplace/blob/HEAD/plugins/qa-hunter/agents/qa-explorer.md))
- **qa-explorer-agent**: Exploratory QA agent. Dispatches the embedded qa-crawler (at .claude/tools/qa-crawler/) against an existing web app to build a structured knowledge base — pages, locators, forms, flows, risks — that downstream QA agents read before authoring tests. Wraps the crawler; does not author test cases itself. Supports `purpose: knowledge` today; `purpose: vision` reserved for future crawler extension. ([tandt53/todo-ai-redesign](https://github.com/tandt53/todo-ai-redesign/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/qa-explorer-agent.md))
- **qa-explorer-ui**: Browser-based QA agent that tests the Jarble frontend via Playwright MCP. Given dynamic test goals, it navigates the app, interacts with UI elements using the accessibility tree (not CSS selectors), captures screenshots, and reports issues. Self-healing — adapts when UI changes because it understands intent, not selectors. Examples: - User: "Test the deployment wizard end-to-end" Assistant: "I will navigate to the wizard, fill out each step, and verify deployment creation." - User: "Verify the homepage loads correctly" Assistant: "I will navigate to /, check all sections render, and capture a… ([Bretthb/jarble](https://github.com/Bretthb/jarble/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/qa-explorer-ui.md) · ★1)

- **qa-eyes**: Агент QA «глазами» (ORCHESTRATOR §4) — проходит сквозной сценарий через системы на своём стенде compose или на MSW, снимает 1440 и 390 px с axe в docs/screens/&lt;задача&gt;/ и возвращает находки одной волной. - **qa-eyes**: The eyes pass of a QA verification. Looks at each new or changed screen at full size, one at a time, as the person who will use it, and answers fixed questions about it in writing in qa/&lt;slug&gt;/eyes.md. Sees only the brief (eyes-prompt.md) and the screenshots it lists, never the code, the spec or summary.md. Dispatched only by the qa skill with the brief `qa eyes prompt` wrote, never proactively. Never runs tests, never reads source, never judges the PR. ([ztsia/workflow](https://github.com/ztsia/workflow/blob/HEAD/plugins/workflow/agents/qa-eyes.md))

- **qa-failure-reproducer**: Reduces one concrete bug report or deterministic failure to a minimal, executed, failing test, and later reruns that unchanged test after the parent fixes the bug. Use when a defect has expected vs observed behavior and fails every time. Not for intermittent failures, multi-step workflow state bugs, API contract mismatches, regression planning, or writing product fixes. - **qa-functional**: Senior/Lead functional QA engineer for ANY project. Use to QA a ticket, feature or build end-to-end — requirement and gap analysis, business-rule extraction, manual test design (happy path, negative, boundary, workflow, role/permission, regression), codebase-first verification, defect validation, and a QA report with a READY / NOT READY recommendation. - **qa-housekeeping**: Runs artifact-retention or workspace-index in an isolated context — the mechanical end-of-run upkeep of the QA workspace. - **qa-hunter**: Read-only vulnerability finder for a security/QA audit, parameterized by domain (authentication, authorization/tenant isolation, input/injection, and others). Given a domain brief, the recon map and a diff or code scope, it traces attacker-controlled input to sinks and produces CANDIDATES — file:line, the hypothesis, a data-flow trace, and a proposed reproduction — never verified findings. It reads code only; it never runs a test suite, never probes a live target, never writes to the target. Used by /bymax-qa:audit, run several in parallel (capped at four), one per domain. ([bymaxone/bymax-agent-kit](https://github.com/bymaxone/bymax-agent-kit/blob/HEAD/codex/plugins/bymax-codex/references/upstream/bymax-qa/agents/qa-hunter.md) · 2 copies)
- **qa-i18n-quality**: Checks translation completeness across the trilingual UI (ka/ru/en TypeScript translation modules), hardcoded strings, dead code, unused imports, console.log statements, and performance anti-patterns. - **qa-i18n-quality**: Checks translation completeness for LiverRa's active locale triad (en/ru/ka with de as retained-fallback), hardcoded strings, dead code, unused imports, console.log statements, and performance anti-patterns. - **qa-qc**: QA/QC engineer. - **qa-quality-advocate**: Speaks only for the end user of the product in the adversarial QA loop and holds a hard user-harm veto that no AI can clear. Reviews blind, receiving neutral facts about the product and its known behavior, with the worker's rationale stripped out. Spawn during the Adversary Review step of the adversarial-qa workflow. Never writes tests. ([paulthorson/agentic-governance](https://github.com/paulthorson/agentic-governance/blob/HEAD/agents/qa-quality-advocate.md) · ★4)

- **qa-quality-engineer**: Senior QA engineer. Writes test strategy, edge cases, regression scenarios, integration tests. You also produce a manual verification plan for the human reviewer. ([shipwrights/core](https://github.com/shipwrights/core/blob/HEAD/agents/qa-quality-engineer.md) · ★2)

- **qa-quincy**: 資深 QA Quincy。負責功能測試、自動化測試（Playwright E2E、JMeter 效能、Postman API）。與 Quinn 互相 review 後整合。由 Jamie 召喚。 ([daidai0825/Stock](https://github.com/daidai0825/Stock/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/qa-quincy.md))

- **qa-ready**: Quality gate for GitHub issue definitions. Checks that an issue is well-defined enough for a bot to implement without ambiguity, then adds `qa:ready`. - **qa-recon**: Read-only reconnaissance agent for a security/QA audit. Given a project root, it detects the stack (language, HTTP framework, ORM/DB, cache, auth, telemetry, frontend), inventories every mounted HTTP route with its guards and public/authenticated status, and lists the trust boundaries. Returns a structured map as data — it finds and describes, it does not judge or report findings. Used by /bymax-qa:audit for the recon phase on codebases too large to read in one pass. ([bymaxone/bymax-agent-kit](https://github.com/bymaxone/bymax-agent-kit/blob/HEAD/codex/plugins/bymax-codex/references/upstream/bymax-qa/agents/qa-recon.md) · 2 copies)
- **qa-reconciler**: You created the QA synthesis by cross-verifying findings from two independent testers. Now both testers have reviewed your assessment and responded. - **qa-recorder**: Records manual browser interactions and generates natural language test scripts with rich locator metadata and assertion capture. - **qa-red-team**: Writes integration and chaos tests (feed drops, broker rejects, partial fills, halts, stale quotes, Jev timeouts, duplicate signals, clock skew, IB Gateway restarts). ([beatsbymaj/orderflowbot](https://github.com/beatsbymaj/orderflowbot/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/qa-red-team.md))
- **qa-red-team-auditor**: Red Team scientific-validity auditor for the Aral Saxaul V6 salinity-risk screening pipeline.
